Use when tasked with evaluating batching economics, delivery frequency optimization, or dispatch cycle decisions. - **infusion-batch-analysis**: Computes and compares annual margin/revenue between two home infusion delivery batch cycles (e.g., 7-day vs 14-day) using a JSON therapy catalog, CSV patient overrides with revision control, per-patient delivery payment CSV, and bag supply cost CSV. Use when tasked with evaluating infusion delivery batching economics, home infusion margin optimization, or delivery frequency decisions. Distinct from cycle-margin-analysis: uses per-therapy patient counts, dose-mg-per-day drug costing, per-patient delivery payments, and alias-based entity matching.
